\hskip 17.07164pt=\sum\limits_{j=1}^{q}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{r_{j}-2}J^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,j-1},n_{1}+i,n_{2,\cdots,p},r_{j}-i-1,r_{j+1,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,j-1},m_{1,\cdots,p},s_{j,\cdots,q})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum\limits_{i=0}^{r_{q+1}-2}J^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q},n_{1}+i,n_{2,\cdots,p},r_{q+1}-i-1;s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n_{1}-1}F^{-1}(i,r_{1,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}+n_{1}-i-1,n_{2,\cdots,p};s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{m_{j}-1}G^{-1}(n_{1,\cdots,j},r_{1,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},i,s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{j}-i,m_{j+1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[G^{-1}(n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),K^{-1}(r_{2,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]

\hskip 17.07164pt=-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{m_{j}-1}G^{-1}(n_{1,\cdots,j},r_{2,\cdots,q},n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},s_{1}+1,s_{2,\cdots,q-1},s_{q}+m_{j}-i-1,m_{j+1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum\limits_{j=2}^{q}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{r_{j}-2}K^{-1}(r_{2,\cdots,j-1},n_{1}+i,n_{2,\cdots,p},r_{j}-i-1,r_{j+1,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,j-1},m_{1,\cdots,p},s_{j,\cdots,q}),

\diamondsuit

[H^{-1}(n_{0,\cdots,p+1};m_{1,\cdots,p}),H^{-1}(r_{0,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]

\hskip 17.07164pt=-\sum\limits_{i=1}^{r_{1}}H^{-1}(n_{0}+r_{0}+i,n_{1,\cdots,p},n_{p+1}+r_{1}-i,r_{2,\cdots,q+1};m_{1,\cdots,p},s_{1,\cdots,q})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n_{1}}H^{-1}(n_{0}+r_{0}+i,r_{1,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}+n_{1}-i,n_{2,\cdots,p+1};s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[H^{-1}(n_{0,\cdots,p+1};m_{1,\cdots,p}),I^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]

\hskip 17.07164pt=\sum\limits_{i=0}^{r_{1}-1}H^{-1}(n_{0}+i,n_{1,\cdots,p},n_{p+1}+r_{1}-i-1,r_{2,\cdots,q+1};m_{1,\cdots,p},s_{1,\cdots,q})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p+1}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{n_{j}-1}H^{-1}(n_{0,\cdots,j-1},r_{1}+i,r_{2,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}+n_{j}-i-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p+1};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{j,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[H^{-1}(n_{0,\cdots,p+1};m_{1,\cdots,p}),J^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]

\hskip 17.07164pt=-\sum\limits_{i=1}^{r_{1}}H^{-1}(n_{0}+i,n_{1,\cdots,p},n_{p+1}+r_{1}-i,r_{2,\cdots,q+1};m_{1,\cdots,p},s_{1,\cdots,q})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n_{1}}H^{-1}(n_{0}+i,r_{1,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}+n_{1}-i,n_{2,\cdots,p+1};s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{m_{j}-1}H^{-1}(n_{0,\cdots,j},r_{1,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p+1};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},i,s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{j}-i,m_{j+1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[H^{-1}(n_{0,\cdots,p+1};m_{1,\cdots,p}),K^{-1}(r_{2,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]

\hskip 17.07164pt=-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{m_{j}-1}H^{-1}(n_{0,\cdots,j},r_{2,\cdots,q},n_{j+1,\cdots,p+1};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},s_{1}+i,s_{2,\cdots,q-1},s_{q}+m_{j}-i-1,m_{j+1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[I^{-1}(n_{1,\cdots,p+1};m_{1,\cdots,p}),I^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]

\hskip 17.07164pt=\sum\limits_{j=1}^{q+1}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{r_{j}-1}I^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,j-1},n_{1}+i,n_{2,\cdots,p},n_{p+1}+r_{j}-i-1,r_{j+1,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,j-1},m_{1,\cdots,p},s_{j,\cdots,q})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p+1}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{n_{j}-1}I^{-1}(n_{1,\cdots,j-1},r_{1}+i,r_{2,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}+n_{j}-i-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p+1};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{j,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[I^{-1}(n_{1,\cdots,p+1};m_{1,\cdots,p}),J^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]

\hskip 17.07164pt=\sum\limits_{j=1}^{q}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{r_{j}-1}J^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,j-1},n_{1}+i,n_{2,\cdots,p},n_{p+1}+r_{j}-i-1,r_{j+1,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,j-1},m_{1,\cdots,p},s_{j,\cdots,q})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum\limits_{i=0}^{r_{q+1}-2}J^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q},n_{1}+i,n_{2,\cdots,p},n_{p+1}+r_{q+1}-i-1;s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n_{1}-1}H^{-1}(i,r_{1,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}+n_{1}-i-1,n_{2,\cdots,p+1};s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{m_{j}-1}I^{-1}(n_{1,\cdots,j},r_{1,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p+1};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},i,s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{j}-i,m_{j+1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[I^{-1}(n_{1,\cdots,p+1};m_{1,\cdots,p}),K^{-1}(r_{2,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]

\hskip 17.07164pt=-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{m_{j}-1}I^{-1}(n_{1,\cdots,j},r_{2,\cdots,q},n_{j+1,\cdots,p+1};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},s_{1}+i,s_{2,\cdots,q-1},s_{q}+m_{j}-i-1,m_{j+1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum\limits_{j=2}^{q}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{r_{j}-1}K^{-1}(r_{2,\cdots,j-1},n_{1}+i,n_{2,\cdots,p},n_{p+1}+r_{j}-i-1,r_{j+1,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,j-1},m_{1,\cdots,p},s_{j,\cdots,q}),

\diamondsuit

[J^{-1}(n_{1,\cdots,p+1};m_{1,\cdots,p}),J^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]

\hskip 17.07164pt=-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{q}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{s_{j}-1}J^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,j},n_{1,\cdots,p},n_{p+1}-1,r_{j+1,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,j-1},i,m_{1,\cdots,p},s_{j}-i,s_{j+1,\cdots,q})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{m_{j}-1}J^{-1}(n_{1,\cdots,j},r_{1,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p+1};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},i,s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{j}-i,m_{j+1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{i=1}^{r_{1}}H^{-1}(i,n_{1,\cdots,p},n_{p+1}+r_{1}-i,r_{2,\cdots,q+1};m_{1,\cdots,p},s_{1,\cdots,q})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n_{1}}H^{-1}(i,r_{1,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}+n_{1}-i,n_{2,\cdots,p+1};s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[J^{-1}(n_{1,\cdots,p+1};m_{1,\cdots,p}),K^{-1}(r_{2,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]

\hskip 17.07164pt=-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{m_{j}-1}J^{-1}(n_{1,\cdots,j},r_{2,\cdots,q},n_{j+1,\cdots,p+1};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},s_{1}+i,s_{2,\cdots,q-1},s_{q}+m_{j}-i-1,m_{j+1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{q}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{s_{j}-1}K^{-1}(r_{2,\cdots,j},n_{1,\cdots,p},n_{p+1}-1,r_{j+1,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,j-1},i,m_{1,\cdots,p},s_{j}-i,s_{j+1,\cdots,q}),

\diamondsuit

[K^{-1}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),K^{-1}(r_{2,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]

\hskip 17.07164pt=\sum\limits_{j=1}^{q}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{s_{j}-1}K^{-1}(r_{2,\cdots,j},n_{2,\cdots,p},r_{j+1,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,j-1},m_{1}+i,m_{2,\cdots,p-1},m_{p}+s_{j}-i-1,s_{j+1,\cdots,q})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{m_{j}-1}K^{-1}(n_{2,\cdots,j},r_{2,\cdots,q},n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},s_{1}+i,s_{2,\cdots,q-1},s_{q}+m_{j}-i-1,m_{j+1,\cdots,p}).

Next, we proceed to compute the Gerstenhaber bracket on the component
\HH^{-2}(A)\otimes\HH^{-1}(A):

[-,-]:\HH^{-2}(A)\otimes\HH^{-1}(A)\to\HH^{-2}(A).

Let
f\in\HH^{-2}(A)
and
g\in\HH^{-1}(A)
be the cochains defined by

f:\left\{\begin{array}[]{rcl}1\otimes(x+y)x\otimes 1&\mapsto&a\\
1\otimes xz\otimes 1&\mapsto&b\\
1\otimes zy\otimes 1&\mapsto&c\end{array}\right.\quad\text{and}\quad g:\left\{\begin{array}[]{rcl}1\otimes x\otimes 1&\mapsto&u\\
1\otimes y\otimes 1&\mapsto&v\\
1\otimes z\otimes 1&\mapsto&w,\end{array}\right.

analogous to the case of the previous component, we obtain their Gerstenhaber bracket
[f,g]\in\HH^{-2}(A)
defined as follows:

[f,g](1\otimes(x+y)x\otimes 1)=(f\circ\pi)\Big(1\otimes(u+v)\otimes x\otimes 1+1\otimes(x+y)\otimes u\otimes 1\Big)-(g\circ\pi)\Big(1\otimes a\otimes 1\Big),

[f,g](1\otimes xz\otimes 1)=(f\circ\pi)\Big(1\otimes u\otimes z\otimes 1+1\otimes x\otimes w\otimes 1\Big)-(g\circ\pi)\Big(1\otimes b\otimes 1\Big),

and

[f,g](1\otimes zy\otimes 1)=(f\circ\pi)\Big(1\otimes w\otimes y\otimes 1+1\otimes z\otimes v\otimes 1\Big)-(g\circ\pi)\Big(1\otimes c\otimes 1\Big).

Proposition 9.2
.

For basis elements
X\in\HH^{-2}(A)
(from Proposition
4.3
) and
Y\in\HH^{-1}(A)
(from Proposition
4.2
), the Gerstenhaber bracket
[X,Y]
is given by the following explicit formulas.

Convention.
In the following equations, we set
B^{-2}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})=0
if any
n_{i}\leq 0
or
m_{j}\leq 0
for
i,j=1,\cdots,p
.

\diamondsuit

[A^{-2},A^{-1}]=A^{-2},

\diamondsuit

[A^{-2},\alpha]=0,
for all basis elements
\alpha
of
\HH^{-1}(A)
other than
A^{-1}
,

\diamondsuit

[B^{-2}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),A^{-1}]=-(\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p}n_{i}+p-2)B^{-2}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[B^{-2}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),B^{-1}(n)]=0,

\diamondsuit

[B^{-2}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),C^{-1}(n+1)]

\hskip 17.07164pt=-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{m_{j}-1}B^{-2}(n_{2,\cdots,j},n,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,j-1},i,m_{j}-i,m_{j+1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[B^{-2}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),D^{-1}(n,i)]=0,

\diamondsuit

[B^{-2}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),E^{-1}(n)]=-\sum\limits_{j=2}^{p}n_{j}B^{-2}(n_{2,\cdots,j-1},n_{j}+n,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[B^{-2}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),F^{-1}(r_{0,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=0,

\diamondsuit

[B^{-2}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),G^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]

\hskip 17.07164pt=-\sum\limits_{j=2}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{n_{j}-2}B^{-2}(n_{2,\cdots,j-1},r_{1}+i,r_{2,\cdots,q},n_{j}-i-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{j,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[B^{-2}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),H^{-1}(r_{0,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=0,

\diamondsuit

[B^{-2}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),I^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]

\hskip 17.07164pt=-\sum\limits_{j=2}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{n_{j}-1}B^{-2}(n_{2,\cdots,j-1},r_{1}+i,r_{2,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}+n_{j}-i-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{j,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[B^{-2}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),J^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]

\hskip 17.07164pt=-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{m_{j}-1}B^{-2}(n_{2,\cdots,j},r_{1,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},i,s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{j}-i,m_{j+1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[B^{-2}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),K^{-1}(r_{2,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]

\hskip 17.07164pt=-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{m_{j}-1}B^{-2}(n_{2,\cdots,j},r_{2,\cdots,q},n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},s_{1}+i,s_{2,\cdots,q-1},s_{q}+m_{j}-i-1,m_{j+1,\cdots,p}).

Finally, we are left with the computation of the Gerstenhaber bracket on
\HH^{-4}(A)\otimes\HH^{-1}(A):

[-,-]:\HH^{-4}(A)\otimes\HH^{-1}(A)\to\HH^{-4}(A).

Let
f\in\HH^{-4}(A)
and
g\in\HH^{-1}(A)
be the cochains defined by

f:(x+y)xzy\mapsto a\quad\text{and}\quad g:\left\{\begin{array}[]{rcl}1\otimes x\otimes 1&\mapsto&u\\
1\otimes y\otimes 1&\mapsto&v\\
1\otimes z\otimes 1&\mapsto&w,\end{array}\right.

analogous to the case of the first component, we obtain their Gerstenhaber bracket
[f,g]\in\HH^{-4}(A)
defined as follows:

\begin{array}[]{l}\vskip 2.84526pt[f,g](1\otimes(x+y)xzy\otimes 1)\\
\vskip 2.84526pt\hskip 17.07164pt=(f\circ\pi)\Big(1\otimes(u+v)\otimes x\otimes z\otimes y\otimes 1\Big)+(f\circ\pi)\Big(1\otimes(x+y)\otimes u\otimes z\otimes y\otimes 1\Big)\\
\vskip 2.84526pt\hskip 28.45274pt+(f\circ\pi)\Big(1\otimes(x+y)\otimes x\otimes w\otimes y\otimes 1\Big)+(f\circ\pi)\Big(1\otimes(x+y)\otimes x\otimes z\otimes v\otimes 1\Big)\\
\hskip 28.45274pt-(g\circ\pi)\Big(1\otimes a\otimes 1\Big).\end{array}

Proposition 9.3
.

For basis elements
X\in\HH^{-4}(A)
(from Proposition
4.5
) and
Y\in\HH^{-1}(A)
(from Proposition
4.2
), the Gerstenhaber bracket
[X,Y]
is given by the following explicit formulas.

Convention.
In the following equations, we set
C^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})=0
if any
n_{i}\leq 0
or
m_{j}\leq 0
for
i,j=1,\cdots,p
, and likewise for
D^{-4},E^{-4},F^{-4},G^{-4},H^{-4}.

\diamondsuit

[A^{-4},A^{-1}]=3A^{-4},

\diamondsuit

[A^{-4},B^{-1}(m)]=-B^{-4}(m-1),

\diamondsuit

[A^{-4},C^{-1}(m)]=B^{-4}(m-1),

\diamondsuit

[A^{-4},D^{-1}(m,i)]=0,

\diamondsuit

[A^{-4},E^{-1}(m)]=B^{-4}(m-1),

\diamondsuit

[A^{-4},F^{-1}(r_{0,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=C^{-4}(r_{0,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})+D^{-4}(r_{0}-1,r_{1,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})+\delta_{r_{0},1}F^{-4}(r_{1,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q}),

\diamondsuit

[A^{-4},G^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=-E^{-4}(r_{1}-1,r_{2,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})-F^{-4}(r_{1}-2,r_{2,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q}),

\diamondsuit

[A^{-4},H^{-1}(r_{0,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=\delta_{r_{q+1},1}D^{-4}(r_{0,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q}),

\diamondsuit

[A^{-4},I^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=\delta_{r_{q+1},1}F^{-4}(r_{1}-1,r_{2,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q}),

\diamondsuit

[A^{-4},J^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=\delta_{r_{q+1},1}F^{-4}(r_{1,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q}),

\diamondsuit

[A^{-4},K^{-1}(r_{2,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=\delta_{q,1}\delta_{s_{1},1}A^{-4},

\diamondsuit

[B^{-4}(n),A^{-1}]=-(n-3)B^{-4},

\diamondsuit

[B^{-4}(n),B^{-1}(m)]=-nB^{-4}(m+n),

\diamondsuit

[B^{-4}(n),C^{-1}(m)]=0,

\diamondsuit

[B^{-4}(n),D^{-1}(m,i)]=0,

\diamondsuit

[B^{-4}(n),E^{-1}(m)]=0,

\diamondsuit

[B^{-4}(n),F^{-1}(r_{0,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=-D^{-4}(n+r_{0},r_{1,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q}),

\diamondsuit

[B^{-4}(n),G^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=D^{-4}(n,r_{1}-1,r_{2,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q}),

\diamondsuit

[B^{-4}(n),H^{-1}(r_{0,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=0,

\diamondsuit

[B^{-4}(n),I^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=0,

\diamondsuit

[B^{-4}(n),J^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=0,

\diamondsuit

[B^{-4}(n),K^{-1}(r_{2,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=\delta_{q,1}\delta_{s_{1},1}B^{-4}(n),

\diamondsuit

[C^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),A^{-1}]=-(\sum\limits_{i=0}^{p}n_{i}+p-3)C^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[C^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),B^{-1}(m)]=-\delta_{m,1}D^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})-(n_{0}+1)C^{-4}(n_{0}+m,n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n_{1}-1}C^{-4}(n_{0}+m+i,n_{1}-i,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[C^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),C^{-1}(m)]=C^{-4}(n_{0}+m,n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n_{1}-1}C^{-4}(n_{0}+m+i,n_{1}-i,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})-\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n_{1}}C^{-4}(n_{0}+i,n_{1}+m-i,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{m_{j}-1}C^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,j},m-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},i,m_{j}-i,m_{j+1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[C^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),D^{-1}(m,i)]=C^{-4}(m+n_{0},n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{n_{1}-1}C^{-4}(n_{0}+m+j,n_{1}-j,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{n_{1}}C^{-4}(n_{0}+i+j,m+n_{1}-i-j,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[C^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),E^{-1}(m)]=\sum\limits_{i=0}^{n_{1}-1}C^{-4}(n_{0}+m+i,n_{1}-i,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{i=0}^{p}n_{i}C^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,i-1},n_{i}+m,n_{i+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[C^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),F^{-1}(r_{0,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=\delta_{r_{0},1}D^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p},r_{1,\cdots,q};m_{1,\cdots,p},s_{1,\cdots,q})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n_{1}-1}C^{-4}(r_{0}+n_{0}+i,r_{1,\cdots,q},n_{1}-i,n_{2,\cdots,p};s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[C^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),G^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=\delta_{r_{1},1}D^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p},r_{2,\cdots,q};m_{1,\cdots,p-1},m_{p}+s_{1},s_{2,\cdots,q})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{n_{j}-2}C^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,j-1},r_{1}+i,r_{2,\cdots,q},n_{j}-i-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{j,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[C^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p+1};m_{1,\cdots,p}),H^{-1}(r_{0,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]

\hskip 17.07164pt=\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n_{1}}C^{-4}(n_{0}+r_{0}+i,r_{1,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}+n_{1}-i,n_{2,\cdots,p};s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[C^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),I^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]

\hskip 17.07164pt=-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{n_{j}-1}C^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,j-1},r_{1}+i,r_{2,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}+n_{j}-i-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{j,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[C^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),J^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]

\hskip 17.07164pt=\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n_{1}}C^{-4}(n_{0}+i,r_{1,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}+n_{1}-i,n_{2,\cdots,p};s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{1,\cdots,p})

\vskip 2.84526pt\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{m_{j}-1}C^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,j},r_{1,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},i,s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{j}-i,m_{j+1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\delta_{r_{q+1},1}D^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p},r_{1,\cdots,q};m_{1,\cdots,p},s_{1,\cdots,q}),

\diamondsuit

[C^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),K^{-1}(r_{2,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=\delta_{q,1}\delta_{s_{1},1}C^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{m_{j}-1}C^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,j},r_{2,\cdots,q},n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},s_{1}+i,s_{2,\cdots,q-1},s_{q}+m_{j}-i-1,m_{j+1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[D^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),A^{-1}]=-(\sum\limits_{i=0}^{p}n_{i}+p-2)D^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[D^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),B^{-1}(m)]=-(n_{0}+1)D^{-4}(n_{0}+m,n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n_{1}-1}D^{-4}(n_{0}+m+i,n_{1}-i,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[D^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),C^{-1}(m)]=D^{-4}(n_{0}+m,n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n_{1}-1}D^{-4}(n_{0}+m+i,n_{1}-i,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})-\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n_{1}}D^{-4}(n_{0}+i,n_{1}+m-i,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{m_{j}-1}D^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,j},m-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},i,m_{j}-i,m_{j+1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[D^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),D^{-1}(m,i)]=D^{-4}(m+n_{0},n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{n_{1}-1}D^{-4}(n_{0}+m+j,n_{1}-j,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{n_{1}}D^{-4}(n_{0}+i+j,m+n_{1}-i-j,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[D^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),E^{-1}(m)]=\sum\limits_{i=0}^{n_{1}-1}D^{-4}(n_{0}+m+i,n_{1}-i,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{i=0}^{p}n_{i}D^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,i-1},n_{i}+m,n_{i+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[D^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),F^{-1}(r_{0,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]

\hskip 28.45274pt=\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n_{1}-1}D^{-4}(r_{0}+n_{0}+i,r_{1,\cdots,q},n_{1}-i,n_{2,\cdots,p};s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[D^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),G^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=D^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p},r_{1}-1,r_{2,\cdots,q};m_{1,\cdots,p},s_{1,\cdots,q})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{n_{j}-2}D^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,j-1},r_{1}+i,r_{2,\cdots,q},n_{j}-i-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{j,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[D^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),H^{-1}(r_{0,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]

\hskip 17.07164pt=\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n_{1}}D^{-4}(n_{0}+r_{0}+i,r_{1,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}+n_{1}-i,n_{2,\cdots,p};s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[D^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),I^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]

\hskip 17.07164pt=-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{n_{j}-1}D^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,j-1},r_{1}+i,r_{2,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}+n_{j}-i-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{j,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[D^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),J^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]

\hskip 17.07164pt=\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n_{1}}D^{-4}(n_{0}+i,r_{1,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}+n_{1}-i,n_{2,\cdots,p};s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{m_{j}-1}D^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,j},r_{1,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},i,s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{j}-i,m_{j+1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[D^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),K^{-1}(r_{2,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=\delta_{q,1}\delta_{s_{1},1}D^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{m_{j}-1}D^{-4}(n_{0,\cdots,j},r_{2,\cdots,q},n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},s_{1}+i,s_{2,\cdots,q-1},s_{q}+m_{j}-i-1,m_{j+1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[E^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),A^{-1}]=-(\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p}n_{i}+p-3)E^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[E^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),B^{-1}(m)]=-\delta_{m,1}F^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{i=0}^{n_{1}-1}D^{-4}(m+i,n_{1}-i,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[E^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),C^{-1}(m)]=C^{-4}(m,n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n_{1}-1}C^{-4}(m+i,n_{1}-i,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})-\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n_{1}}C^{-4}(i,n_{1}+m-i,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{m_{j}-1}E^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,j},m-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},i,m_{j}-i,m_{j+1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[E^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),D^{-1}(m,i)]=C^{-4}(m,n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{n_{1}-1}C^{-4}(m+j,n_{1}-j,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{n_{1}}C^{-4}(i+j,m+n_{1}-i-j,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[E^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),E^{-1}(m)]=\sum\limits_{i=0}^{n_{1}-1}C^{-4}(m+i,n_{1}-i,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p}n_{i}E^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,i-1},n_{i}+m,n_{i+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[E^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),F^{-1}(r_{0,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=\delta_{r_{0},1}E^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p},r_{1,\cdots,q};m_{1,\cdots,p},s_{1,\cdots,q})

\hskip 28.45274pt\sum\limits_{i=0}^{n_{1}-1}C^{-4}(r_{0}+i,r_{1,\cdots,q},n_{1}-i,n_{2,\cdots,p};s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

\vskip 2.84526pt[E^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),G^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=E^{-4}(r_{1}-1,r_{2,\cdots,q},n_{1,\cdots,p};s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\delta_{r_{1},1}F^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p},r_{2,\cdots,q};m_{1,\cdots,p-1},m_{p}+s_{1},s_{2,\cdots,q})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{n_{j}-2}E^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,j-1},r_{1}+i,r_{2,\cdots,q},n_{j}-i-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{j,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[E^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),H^{-1}(r_{0,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]

\hskip 17.07164pt=\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n_{1}}C^{-4}(r_{0}+i,r_{1,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}+n_{1}-i,n_{2,\cdots,p};s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[E^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),I^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]

\hskip 17.07164pt=-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{n_{j}-1}E^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,j-1},r_{1}+i,r_{2,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}+n_{j}-i-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{j,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[E^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),J^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]

\hskip 17.07164pt=\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n_{1}}C^{-4}(i,r_{1,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}+n_{1}-i,n_{2,\cdots,p};s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{1,\cdots,p})

\vskip 2.84526pt\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{m_{j}-1}E^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,j},r_{1,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},i,s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{j}-i,m_{j+1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\delta_{r_{q+1},1}F^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p},r_{1,\cdots,q};m_{1,\cdots,p},s_{1,\cdots,q}),

\diamondsuit

[E^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),K^{-1}(r_{2,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=\delta_{q,1}\delta_{s_{1},1}E^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{m_{j}-1}E^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,j},r_{2,\cdots,q},n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},s_{1}+i,s_{2,\cdots,q-1},s_{q}+m_{j}-i-1,m_{j+1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[F^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),A^{-1}]=-(\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p}n_{i}+p-2)F^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[F^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),B^{-1}(m)]=-\sum\limits_{i=0}^{n_{1}-1}F^{-4}(m+i,n_{1}-i,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[F^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),C^{-1}(m)]=D^{-4}(m,n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n_{1}-1}D^{-4}(m+i,n_{1}-i,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})-\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n_{1}}D^{-4}(i,n_{1}+m-i,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{m_{j}-1}F^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,j},m-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},i,m_{j}-i,m_{j+1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[F^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),D^{-1}(m,i)]=D^{-4}(m,n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{n_{1}-1}D^{-4}(m+j,n_{1}-j,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{n_{1}}D^{-4}(i+j,m+n_{1}-i-j,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[F^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),E^{-1}(m)]=\sum\limits_{i=0}^{n_{1}-1}D^{-4}(m+i,n_{1}-i,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{i=1}^{p}n_{i}F^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,i-1},n_{i}+m,n_{i+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[F^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),F^{-1}(r_{0,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=\sum\limits_{i=0}^{n_{1}-1}D^{-4}(r_{0}+i,r_{1,\cdots,q},n_{1}-i,n_{2,\cdots,p};s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

\vskip 2.84526pt[F^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),G^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=F^{-4}(r_{1}-1,r_{2,\cdots,q},n_{1,\cdots,p};s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt+F^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p},r_{1}-1,r_{2,\cdots,q};m_{1,\cdots,p},s_{1,\cdots,q})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{n_{j}-2}F^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,j-1},r_{1}+i,r_{2,\cdots,q},n_{j}-i-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{j,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[F^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),H^{-1}(r_{0,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]

\hskip 17.07164pt=\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n_{1}}D^{-4}(r_{0}+i,r_{1,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}+n_{1}-i,n_{2,\cdots,p};s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[F^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),I^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]

\hskip 17.07164pt=-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{n_{j}-1}F^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,j-1},r_{1}+i,r_{2,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}+n_{j}-i-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{j,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

\vskip 2.84526pt[F^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),J^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]

\hskip 17.07164pt=\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n_{1}}D^{-4}(i,r_{1,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}+n_{1}-i,n_{2,\cdots,p};s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{m_{j}-1}F^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,j},r_{1,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},i,s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{j}-i,m_{j+1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[F^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),K^{-1}(r_{2,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=\delta_{q,1}\delta_{s_{1},1}F^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{m_{j}-1}F^{-4}(n_{1,\cdots,j},r_{2,\cdots,q},n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},s_{1}+i,s_{2,\cdots,q-1},s_{q}+m_{j}-i-1,m_{j+1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[G^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),A^{-1}]=-(\sum\limits_{i=2}^{p}n_{i}+p-4)G^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[G^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),B^{-1}(m)]=-\delta_{m,1}H^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[G^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),C^{-1}(m)]=E^{-4}(m-1,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{m_{j}-1}G^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,j},m-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},i,m_{j}-i,m_{j+1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[G^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),D^{-1}(m,i)]=-C^{-4}(i,m-i-1,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[G^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),E^{-1}(m)]=E^{-4}(m-1,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{i=2}^{p}n_{i}G^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,i-1},n_{i}+m,n_{i+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[G^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),F^{-1}(r_{0,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=C^{-4}(r_{0,\cdots,q},n_{2,\cdots,p};s_{1,\cdots,q-1},s_{q}+m_{1},m_{2,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\delta_{r_{0},1}H^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p},r_{1,\cdots,q};m_{1,\cdots,p},s_{1,\cdots,q}),

\diamondsuit

\vskip 2.84526pt[G^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),G^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=E^{-4}(r_{1}-1,r_{2,\cdots,q},n_{2,\cdots,p};s_{1,\cdots,q-1},s_{q}+m_{1},m_{2,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\delta_{r_{1},1}H^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p},r_{2,\cdots,q};m_{1,\cdots,p-1},m_{p}+s_{1},s_{2,\cdots,q})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{j=2}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{n_{j}-2}G^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,j-1},r_{1}+i,r_{2,\cdots,q},n_{j}-i-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{j,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

\vskip 2.84526pt[G^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),H^{-1}(r_{0,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=C^{-4}(r_{0,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}-1,n_{2,\cdots,p};s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[G^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),I^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=E^{-4}(r_{1}-1,r_{2,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}-1,n_{2,\cdots,p};s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{j=2}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{n_{j}-1}G^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,j-1},r_{1}+i,r_{2,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}+n_{j}-i-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{j,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[G^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),J^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=E^{-4}(r_{1,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}-1,n_{2,\cdots,p};s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{1,\cdots,p})

\vskip 2.84526pt\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{m_{j}-1}G^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,j},r_{1,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},i,s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{j}-i,m_{j+1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\delta_{r_{q+1},1}H^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p},r_{1,\cdots,q};m_{1,\cdots,p},s_{1,\cdots,q}),

\diamondsuit

[G^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),K^{-1}(r_{2,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=\delta_{q,1}\delta_{s_{1},1}G^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{m_{j}-1}G^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,j},r_{2,\cdots,q},n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},s_{1}+i,s_{2,\cdots,q-1},s_{q}+m_{j}-i-1,m_{j+1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[H^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),A^{-1}]=-(\sum\limits_{i=2}^{p}n_{i}+p-3)H^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[H^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),B^{-1}(m)]=0,

\diamondsuit

[H^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),C^{-1}(m)]=F^{-4}(m-1,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{m_{j}-1}H^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,j},m-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},i,m_{j}-i,m_{j+1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[H^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),D^{-1}(m,i)]=-D^{-4}(i,m-i-1,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[H^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),E^{-1}(m)]=F^{-4}(m-1,n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{i=2}^{p}n_{i}H^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,i-1},n_{i}+m,n_{i+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[H^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),F^{-1}(r_{0,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=D^{-4}(r_{0,\cdots,q},n_{2,\cdots,p};s_{1,\cdots,q-1},s_{q}+m_{1},m_{2,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

\vskip 2.84526pt[H^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),G^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=F^{-4}(r_{1}-1,r_{2,\cdots,q},n_{2,\cdots,p};s_{1,\cdots,q-1},s_{q}+m_{1},m_{2,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt+H^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p},r_{1}-1,r_{2,\cdots,q};m_{1,\cdots,p},s_{1,\cdots,q})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{j=2}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{n_{j}-2}H^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,j-1},r_{1}+i,r_{2,\cdots,q},n_{j}-i-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{j,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[H^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),H^{-1}(r_{0,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=D^{-4}(r_{0,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}-1,n_{2,\cdots,p};s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[H^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),I^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=F^{-4}(r_{1}-1,r_{2,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}-1,n_{2,\cdots,p};s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt-\sum\limits_{j=2}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{n_{j}-1}H^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,j-1},r_{1}+i,r_{2,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}+n_{j}-i-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{j,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[H^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),J^{-1}(r_{1,\cdots,q+1};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=F^{-4}(r_{1,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}-1,n_{2,\cdots,p};s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{1,\cdots,p})

\hskip 28.45274pt+\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{m_{j}-1}H^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,j},r_{1,\cdots,q},r_{q+1}-1,n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},i,s_{1,\cdots,q},m_{j}-i,m_{j+1,\cdots,p}),

\diamondsuit

[H^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p}),K^{-1}(r_{2,\cdots,q};s_{1,\cdots,q})]=\delta_{q,1}\delta_{s_{1},1}H^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,p})

-\sum\limits_{j=1}^{p}\sum\limits_{i=0}^{m_{j}-1}H^{-4}(n_{2,\cdots,j},r_{2,\cdots,q},n_{j+1,\cdots,p};m_{1,\cdots,j-1},s_{1}+i,s_{2,\cdots,q-1},s_{q}+m_{j}-i-1,m_{j+1,\cdots,p}).

Appendix A
The Tamarkin–Tsygan Calculus

In this section, let
\mathbf{k}
be a field of characteristic zero, and let
A
be an augmented associative algebra over
\mathbf{k}
with augmentation
\varepsilon:A\to\mathbf{k}.
Denote
\overline{A}:=\mathrm{coker}(\varepsilon)=A/\mathbf{k}.

The definitions of all the operations of Tamarkin–Tsygan calculus including the cup product, the cap product, the Gerstenhaber bracket and the Connes’ differential are adopted from
[
20
]
.

Definition A.1

(
A
-bimodule)
.

(i)

The
opposite algebra

(A^{\mathrm{op},},\mu_{A^{\mathrm{op}}})
of
(A,\mu_{A})
is defined to be isomorphic to
A
as a
\mathbf{k}
-vector space, and
\mu_{A^{\mathrm{op}}}(a\otimes b)=\mu_{A}(b\otimes a)
.

(ii)

Let
A^{e}:=A\otimes A^{\mathrm{op}}
. An
A^{e}
-left module is said to be an
A
-bimodule
.

Remark A.2
.

(i)

An
A
-bimodule
M
is equivalent to a left and right
A
-module, satisfying the compatibility relation
(a\cdot m)\cdot b=a\cdot(m\cdot b)
for all
a,b\in A,m\in M
.

(ii)

The algebra
A
itself has a natural left
A^{e}
-module structure given by
(b\otimes c)\cdot a:=bac
and a right
A^{e}
-module structure given by
a\cdot(b\otimes c)=cab
given by the multiplications of
A
.

Definition A.3

(Hochschild (Co)homology)
.

(i)

The
Hochschild homology
of
A
is defined to be
\HH_{*}(A):=\Tor^{A^{e}}_{*}(A,A)
, the Tor functor of
A
over
A^{e}
.

(ii)

The
Hochschild cohomology
of
A
is defined to be
\HH^{*}(A):=\Ext^{-*}_{A^{e}}(A,A)
, the Ext functor of
A
over
A^{e}
.
Note that, by convention, the Hochschild cohomology is concentrated in non-positive degrees, which is indicated by the minus sign in the upper index.

Definition A.4

(Bar resolution)
.

For
n\geqslant 0
, let
B_{n}(A,A):=A\otimes\overline{A}^{\otimes n}\otimes A
, with
A^{e}
acting on the sides by left and right multiplications. Additionally, we define
B_{-1}(A,A):=A
.
Then
B_{n}(A,A)
is a free
A^{e}
module. For
n\geqslant 1
, let
d_{n}:B_{n}(A,A)\to B_{n-1}(A,A)
be the
A^{e}
-linear map given by

\begin{array}[]{rcl}a_{0}\otimes a_{1}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{n}\otimes a_{n+1}&\mapsto&\sum_{i=0}^{n}(-1)^{i}a_{0}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{i}a_{i+1}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{n+1}.\end{array}

The augmentation map
d_{0}:B_{0}(A,A)=A\otimes A\to A
is defined to be the multiplication of
A
:

a_{0}\otimes a_{1}\mapsto a_{0}a_{1}.

Then
(B_{\bullet}(A,A),d_{\bullet})
is a free
A^{e}
-resolution of
A
, called the
reduced two-sided bar resolution
of
A
.

Therefore, we can compute the Hochschild (co)homology using the bar resolution. In other words,
\HH_{*}(A)=\Homology_{*}(A\otimes_{A^{e}}B_{\bullet}(A,A))
and
\HH^{*}(A)=\Homology^{-*}(\Hom_{A^{e}}(B_{\bullet}(A,A),A))
.

Definition A.5

(Hochschild (Co)chain Complex)
.

(i)

Let
C_{\bullet}(A):=A\otimes_{A^{e}}B_{\bullet}(A,A)
, equipped with the differential
d_{\bullet}:=\id_{A}\otimes_{A^{e}}d_{\bullet}
, where we abuse notation by using
d_{\bullet}
for both differentials.
Then
(C_{\bullet},d_{\bullet})
forms a chain complex, called the
Hochschild chain complex
of
A
.

(ii)

Similarly, let
C^{\bullet}(A):=\Hom_{A^{e}}(B_{\bullet}(A,A),A)
and
d_{\bullet}^{*}:C^{\bullet}(A)\to C^{\bullet+1}(A)
be the pre-composition by
d_{\bullet}
of
B_{\bullet}(A,A)
, then
(C^{\bullet}(A),d^{*}_{\bullet})
forms a cochain complex, called the
Hochschild cochain complex
of
A
.

Under the identification
C_{n}(A)\cong A\otimes\overline{A}^{\otimes n}
and
C^{n}(A)\cong\Hom(\overline{A}^{\otimes n},A),n\geqslant 0,
the differentials of the above two complexes are given by:

d_{n}(a_{0}\otimes a_{1}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{n})=\sum\limits_{i=0}^{n-1}(-1)^{i}a_{0}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{i}a_{i+1}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{n}+(-1)^{n}a_{n}a_{0}\otimes a_{1}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{n-1}

for
n\geq 0
,

\hskip-312.9803ptd^{n}(f)(a_{1}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{n+1})

\hskip 0.0pt=\sum\limits_{i=1}^{n}(-1)^{i}f(a_{1}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{i}a_{i+1}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{n+1})+a_{1}f(a_{2}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{n+1})+(-1)^{n+1}f(a_{1}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{n})a_{n+1},

for
n\geqslant 1,

a_{0}\in A,a_{1},\cdots,a_{n+1}\in\overline{A},
and
f\in\Hom(\overline{A}^{\otimes n},A),
and

d^{0}(a)(b)=ab-ba

for
a\in A\cong C^{0}(A),b\in\overline{A}.

The Tamarkin–Tsygan calculus consists of operations on Hochschild homology and cohomology that enjoy certain compatibilities with each other. They can be defined on the Hochschild chains and cochains and then transferred on the (co)homology level.

Definition A.6

(Cup Product)
.

The
cup product
on the cochain complex is defined to be

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\cup_{-m,-n}:C^{-m}(A)\otimes C^{-n}(A)&\to&C^{-m-n}(A)\\
f\otimes g&\mapsto&a_{1}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{m+n}\mapsto(-1)^{mn}f(a_{1}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{m})\cdot g(a_{m+1}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{m+n})\end{array}

for
m,n\in\mathbb{N}
. Denote by
\cup_{-m,-n}(f\otimes g)=:f\cup g
.

Proposition A.7
.

[
10
]

The cup product induces to a well-defined, graded-commutative and associative product on the Hochschild cohomology:

\cup:\HH^{*}(A)\otimes\HH^{*}(A)\to\HH^{*}(A)

which makes the Hochschild cohomology
\HH^{*}(A)
a graded commutative associative algebra over
\mathbf{k}
.

Definition A.8
.

Let
f\in C^{m}(A)
and
g\in C^{n}(A)
. Their circle product
f\circ g\in C^{m+n-1}(A)
is defined by

\begin{array}[]{rcl}f\circ g:A^{\otimes(m+n-1)}&\to&A\\
a_{1}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{m+n-1}&\mapsto&\sum_{i=1}^{m}(-1)^{(n-1)(i-1)}f(a_{1}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{i-1}\otimes g(a_{i}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{i+n-1})\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{m+n-1})\end{array}

Let
[f,g]:=f\circ g-(-1)^{(m-1)(n-1)}g\circ f
be the graded-commutator of the operation
\circ
. The bracket
[f,g]
is called the
Gerstenhaber bracket
of
f
and
g
.

Proposition A.9
.

Gerstenhaber bracket induces a well-defined Lie bracket

\begin{array}[]{rcl}[-,-]:\HH^{-m}(A)\otimes\HH^{-n}(A)&\to&\HH^{-n-m+1}(A)\\
f\otimes g&\mapsto&f\circ g-(-1)^{(m-1)(n-1)}g\circ f\end{array}

on
\HH^{*}(A)
of degree
1
, where
m,n\geqslant 0
.

The cup product and Gerstenhaber bracket on Hochschild cohomology are compatible with each other in the following sense:

Definition A.10

(Gerstenhaber Algebra)
.

A
Gerstenhaber algebra
is a triple
(H,\cup,[-,-])
where
H
is a
\mathbb{Z}
-graded vector space over
\mathbf{k}
,
\cup:H\otimes H\to H
is a graded commutative associative product of degree
0
, and
[-,-]:H\otimes H\to H
is a graded Lie bracket of degree
1
, such that
[-,-]
satisfies the graded Leibniz rule with respect to
\cup
:

[f,g\cup h]=[f,g]\cup h+(-1)^{|f|(|g|-1)}g\cup[f,h]

Proposition A.11
.

[
6
,
10
]

For any associative algebra
A
over
\mathbf{k}
, the Hochschild cohomology
\HH^{*}(A)
, together with the cup product and Gerstenhaber bracket defined in Proposition
A.7
and Definition
A.8
, forms a Gerstenhaber algebra.

We have the action of Hochschild cohomology on Hochschild homology, making the homology a module over the cohomology.

Definition A.12

(Cap Product)
.

The
cap product
on the chain by the cochain complex is defined to be

\begin{array}[]{rcl}\cap_{m}^{-n}:C^{-n}(A)\otimes C_{m}(A)&\to&C_{m-n}(A)\\
f\otimes(a_{0}\otimes a_{1}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{m})&\mapsto&(a_{0}f(a_{1}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{n}))\otimes a_{n+1}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{m}\end{array}

for
m,n\in\mathbb{N},m\geqslant n
. Denote
\cap_{m,n}((a_{0}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{m})\otimes f)
by
(a_{0}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{m})\cap f
.

Proposition A.13
.

The cap product extends to a well-defined map:

\cap:\HH^{*}(A)\otimes\HH_{*}(A)\to\HH_{*}(A)

of degree
0
. Moreover,
(\HH_{*}(A),\cap)
is a graded left module over
(\HH^{*}(A),\cup)
and
(\HH^{*}(A),[-,-])
.

Definition A.14

(Connes’ Differential)
.

The
Connes’ differential
on Hochschild chain complex is defined to be

\begin{array}[]{rcl}b_{n}:C_{n}(A)&\to&C_{n+1}(A)\\
a_{0}\otimes a_{1}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{n}&\mapsto&\sum_{i=0}^{n}(-1)^{ni}1\otimes a_{i+1}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{n}\otimes a_{0}\otimes\cdots\otimes a_{i}\end{array}

for
n\geqslant 0
.

Proposition A.15
.

b_{*}
extends to a well-defined differential on Hochschild homology:

b_{n}:\HH_{n}(A)\to\HH_{n+1}(A)

satisfying
b^{2}=0
and

[[b,i_{f}],i_{g}]=i_{[f,g]}

where
f\in\HH^{-p}(A)
,
g\in\HH^{-q}(A)
,
i_{f}=f\cap-:\HH_{*}(A)\to\HH_{*-p}
is the cap product operation by
f
. The brackets on the left denote the graded commutator, and the bracket on the right denotes the Gerstenhaber bracket.

Definition A.16

(Tamarkin–Tsygan Calculus)
.

A
Tamarkin–Tsygan calculus
consists of the following data:

(i)

Two graded vector spaces
\left(H_{*},H^{*}\right)
;

(ii)

A cup product
\cup:H^{*}\otimes H^{*}\to H^{*}
of degree
0
;

(iii)

A cap product
\cap:H^{*}\otimes H_{*}\to H_{*}
of degree
0
;

(iv)

A Gerstenhaber bracket
[-,-]:H^{*}\otimes H^{*}\to H^{*}
of degree
1
;

(v)

A Connes’ differential
B:H_{*}\to H_{*}
of degree 1;

such that

(vi)

(H^{*},\cup,[-,-])
is a Gerstenhaber algebra;

(vii)

H_{*}
with the action
\cap
is a left module over
(H^{*},\cup)
and over
(H^{*},[-,-])
;

(viii)

b
is a differential satisfying
b^{2}=0
and
[[b,i_{f}],i_{g}]=i_{[f,g]}
.

Proposition A.17
.

[
17
,
18
]

The pair
(\HH_{*}(A),\HH^{*}(A))
of Hochschild homology and cohomology of
A
, together with the cup product, the Gerstenhaber bracket, the cap product and the Connes’ differential given in Definitions
A.7
,
A.8
,
A.12
and
A.14
respectively, form a Tamarkin–Tsygan calculus.

d_{1}(\alpha)=0;

c=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}},a=0,b=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z;

c=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},a=0,b=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p-1}}xy^{n_{p}}z^{m_{p}+1};

c=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,a=0,b=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=0;

c=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},a=0,b=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}z;

c=x,a=0,b=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=-zx;

c=xy^{n_{1}},a=0,b=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=xy^{n_{1}}z-zxy^{n_{1}};

c=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},a=0,b=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p-1}}xy^{n_{p}}z^{m_{p}+1}-zxy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}};

c=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,a=0,b=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=-zxy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x;

c=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},a=0,b=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}z-zxy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}};

c=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},a=0,b=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p-1}}xy^{n_{p}}z^{m_{p}+1};

c=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,a=0,b=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=0;

c=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},a=0,b=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}z;

c=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},a=0,b=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p-1}}xy^{n_{p}}z^{m_{p}+1}-z^{m_{1}+1}xy^{n_{2}}z^{m_{2}}\cdots z^{m_{p}};

c=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,a=0,b=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=-z^{m_{1}+1}xy^{n_{2}}z^{m_{2}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x;

c=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},a=0,b=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}z-z^{m_{1}+1}xy^{n_{2}}z^{m_{2}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}};

b=1,a=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=0;

b=y^{n_{0}},a=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=0;

b=y^{n_{0}}x,a=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=y^{n_{0}}xy-y^{n_{0}+1}x;

b=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}},a=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}+1}-y^{n_{0}+1}xy^{n_{1}};

b=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},a=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=-y^{n_{0}+1}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}};

b=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,a=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy-y^{n_{0}+1}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x;

b=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},a=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}+1}-y^{n_{0}+1}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}};

b=x,a=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=xy-yx;

b=xy^{n_{1}},a=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=xy^{n_{1}+1}-yxy^{n_{1}};

b=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},a=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=-yxy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}};

b=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,a=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy-yxy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x;

b=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},a=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}+1}-yxy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}};

b=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},a=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=-y^{n_{1}+1}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}};

b=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,a=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy-y^{n_{1}+1}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x;

b=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},a=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}+1}-y^{n_{1}+1}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}};

b=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},a=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=-yz^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}};

b=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,a=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy-yz^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x;

b=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},a=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}+1}-yz^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}};

a=1,b=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=0;

a=y^{n_{0}},b=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=y^{n_{0}}x-xy^{n_{0}};

a=y^{n_{0}}x,b=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=0;

a=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}},b=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=-y^{n_{0}+n_{1}+1}x+y^{n_{0}+1}xy^{n_{1}};

a=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},b=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x+y^{n_{0}+1}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}};

a=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,b=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=y^{n_{0}+1}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x;

a=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},b=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=y^{n_{0}+1}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}};

a=x,b=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=0;

a=xy^{n_{1}},b=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=-y^{n_{1}+1}x+yxy^{n_{1}};

a=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},b=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x+yxy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}};

a=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,b=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=yxy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x;

a=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},b=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=yxy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}};

a=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},b=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x-xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}};

a=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,b=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=-xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x;

a=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},b=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}};

a=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},b=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x;

a=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,b=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=0;

a=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},b=0,c=0,

d_{1}(\alpha)=0.

By computing the action of
d_{1}^{*}
on the basis
{\mathcal{B}}
of
\Hom(V_{0},A)\cong A,

we obtain the following conclusion.

Lemma B.4
.

The image of the differential
d_{1}^{*}
is generated over
\mathbf{k}
by the elements of the form
d_{1}^{*}(a)
with
a\in B:

a=1,

d_{1}^{*}(a)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}x&\mapsto&0\\
y&\mapsto&0\\
z&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=y^{n_{0}},

d_{1}^{*}(a)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}x&\mapsto&xy^{n_{0}}-y^{n_{0}}x\\
y&\mapsto&0\\
z&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}}z\end{array}\right);

a=y^{n_{0}}x,

d_{1}^{*}(a)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}x&\mapsto&0\\
y&\mapsto&y^{n_{0}+1}x-y^{n_{0}}xy\\
z&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}},

d_{1}^{*}(a)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}x&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}+1}xy^{n_{1}}+y^{n_{0}+n_{1}+1}x\\
y&\mapsto&y^{n_{0}+1}xy^{n_{1}}-y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}+1}\\
z&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z\end{array}\right);

a=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},

d_{1}^{*}(a)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}x&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}+1}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}-y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\\
y&\mapsto&y^{n_{0}+1}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}\\
z&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p-1}}xy^{n_{p}}z^{m_{p}+1}\end{array}\right);

a=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,

d_{1}^{*}(a)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}x&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}+1}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\\
y&\mapsto&y^{n_{0}+1}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x-y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy\\
z&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},

d_{1}^{*}(a)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}x&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}+1}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}\\
y&\mapsto&y^{n_{0}+1}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}-y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}+1}\\
z&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}z\end{array}\right);

a=x,

d_{1}^{*}(a)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}x&\mapsto&0\\
y&\mapsto&yx-xy\\
z&\mapsto&zx\end{array}\right);

a=xy^{n_{1}},

d_{1}^{*}(a)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}x&\mapsto&-yxy^{n_{1}}+y^{n_{1}+1}x\\
y&\mapsto&yxy^{n_{1}}-xy^{n_{1}+1}\\
z&\mapsto&zxy^{n_{1}}-xy^{n_{1}}z\end{array}\right);

a=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},

d_{1}^{*}(a)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}x&\mapsto&-yxy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}-xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\\
y&\mapsto&yxy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}\\
z&\mapsto&zxy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}-xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p-1}}xy^{n_{p}}z^{m_{p}+1}\end{array}\right);

a=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,

d_{1}^{*}(a)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}x&\mapsto&-yxy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\\
y&\mapsto&yxy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x-xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy\\
z&\mapsto&zxy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\end{array}\right);

a=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},

d_{1}^{*}(a)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}x&\mapsto&-yxy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}\\
y&\mapsto&yxy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}-xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}+1}\\
z&\mapsto&zxy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots y^{n_{p+1}}-xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}z\end{array}\right);

a=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},

d_{1}^{*}(a)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}x&\mapsto&xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}-y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\\
y&\mapsto&y^{n_{1}+1}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}\\
z&\mapsto&y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p-1}}xy^{n_{p}}z^{m_{p}+1}\end{array}\right);

a=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,

d_{1}^{*}(a)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}x&\mapsto&xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\\
y&\mapsto&y^{n_{1}+1}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x-y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy\\
z&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},

d_{1}^{*}(a)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}x&\mapsto&xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}\\
y&\mapsto&y^{n_{1}+1}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}-y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}+1}\\
z&\mapsto&-y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}z\end{array}\right);

a=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},

d_{1}^{*}(a)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}x&\mapsto&-z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\\
y&\mapsto&yz^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}\\
z&\mapsto&z^{m_{1}+1}xy^{n_{2}}z^{m_{2}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}-z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p-1}}xy^{n_{p}}z^{m_{p}+1}\end{array}\right);

a=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,

d_{1}^{*}(a)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}x&\mapsto&0\\
y&\mapsto&yz^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x-z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy\\
z&\mapsto&z^{m_{1}+1}xy^{n_{2}}z^{m_{2}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\end{array}\right);

a=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},

d_{1}^{*}(a)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}x&\mapsto&0\\
y&\mapsto&yz^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}-z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}+1}\\
z&\mapsto&z^{m_{1}+1}xy^{n_{2}}z^{m_{2}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}-z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}z\end{array}\right).

Here, the indices run over all integers
p\geqslant 1,
and
n_{0},n_{1},\cdots,n_{p+1},m_{1},\cdots,m_{p}\geqslant 1.

By computing the action of
d_{2}^{*}
on a canonical basis of
\Hom(V_{1},A),
namely the elements

\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}x&\mapsto&a\\
y&\mapsto&0\\
z&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right),\quad\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}x&\mapsto&0\\
y&\mapsto&b\\
z&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right),\quad\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}x&\mapsto&0\\
y&\mapsto&0\\
z&\mapsto&c\end{array}\right),

with
a,b,c\in{\mathcal{B}},
we obtain the following conclusion.

Lemma B.5
.

The image of the differential
d_{2}^{*}
is generated over
\mathbf{k}
by the elements of the form
d_{2}^{*}(f),
where

f=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}x&\mapsto&a\\
y&\mapsto&b\\
z&\mapsto&c\end{array}\right)\in\Hom(V_{1},A)
runs over the following cases:

a=1,b=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&2x+y\\
xz&\mapsto&z\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=y^{n_{0}},b=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&xy^{n_{0}}+y^{n_{0}+1}+y^{n_{0}}x\\
xz&\mapsto&y^{n_{0}}z\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=y^{n_{0}}x,b=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}+1}x\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}},b=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}+n_{1}+1}x\\
xz&\mapsto&y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},b=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\\
xz&\mapsto&y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p-1}}xy^{n_{p}}z^{m_{p}+1}\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,b=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},b=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}z\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=x,b=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&-yx\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=xy^{n_{1}},b=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&-y^{n_{1}+1}x\\
xz&\mapsto&xy^{n_{1}}z\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},b=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\\
xz&\mapsto&xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p-1}}xy^{n_{p}}z^{m_{p}+1}\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,b=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},b=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}z\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},b=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}+y^{n_{1}+1}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}+y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\\
xz&\mapsto&y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p-1}}xy^{n_{p}}z^{m_{p}+1}\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,b=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x+y^{n_{1}+1}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},b=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}+y^{n_{1}+1}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}\\
xz&\mapsto&y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}z\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},b=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&yz^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}+z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\\
xz&\mapsto&z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p-1}}xy^{n_{p}}z^{m_{p}+1}\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,b=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&yz^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},b=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&yz^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}\\
xz&\mapsto&z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}z\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right).

b=1,a=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&x\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&z\end{array}\right);

b=y^{n_{0}},a=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&y^{n_{0}}x\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

b=y^{n_{0}}x,a=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}+1}x\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

b=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}},a=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}+n_{1}+1}x\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

b=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},a=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

b=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,a=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

b=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},a=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

b=x,a=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&-yx\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&zx\end{array}\right);

b=xy^{n_{1}},a=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&-y^{n_{1}+1}x\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&zxy^{n_{1}}\end{array}\right);

b=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},a=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&zxy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}\end{array}\right);

b=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,a=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&zxy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\end{array}\right);

b=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},a=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&zxy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}\end{array}\right);

b=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},a=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

b=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,a=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

b=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},a=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

b=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},a=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&z^{m_{1}+1}xy^{n_{2}}z^{m_{2}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}\end{array}\right);

b=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,a=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&z^{m_{1}+1}xy^{n_{2}}z^{m_{2}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\end{array}\right);

b=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},a=c=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&z^{m_{1}+1}xy^{n_{2}}z^{m_{2}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}\end{array}\right).

c=1,a=b=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&x\\
zy&\mapsto&y\end{array}\right);

c=y^{n_{0}},a=b=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&xy^{n_{0}}\\
zy&\mapsto&y^{n_{0}+1}\end{array}\right);

c=y^{n_{0}}x,a=b=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}+1}x\\
zy&\mapsto&y^{n_{0}}xy\end{array}\right);

c=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}},a=b=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}+1}xy^{n_{1}}\\
zy&\mapsto&y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}+1}\end{array}\right);

c=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},a=b=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}+1}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

c=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,a=b=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}+1}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\\
zy&\mapsto&y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy\end{array}\right);

c=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},a=b=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}+1}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}\\
zy&\mapsto&y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}+1}\end{array}\right);

c=x,a=b=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&-yx\\
zy&\mapsto&xy\end{array}\right);

c=xy^{n_{1}},a=b=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&-yxy^{n_{1}}\\
zy&\mapsto&xy^{n_{1}+1}\end{array}\right);

c=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},a=b=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&-yxy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

c=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,a=b=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&-yxy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\\
zy&\mapsto&xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy\end{array}\right);

c=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},a=b=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&-yxy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}\\
zy&\mapsto&xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}+1}\end{array}\right);

c=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},a=b=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

c=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,a=b=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\\
zy&\mapsto&y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy\end{array}\right);

c=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},a=b=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}\\
zy&\mapsto&y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}+1}\end{array}\right);

c=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},a=b=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

c=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,a=b=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy\end{array}\right);

c=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},a=b=0,

d_{2}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}+1}\end{array}\right).

Here, the indices run over all integers
p\geqslant 1,
and
n_{0},n_{1},\cdots,n_{p+1},m_{1},\cdots,m_{p}\geqslant 1.

By computing the action of
d_{3}^{*}
on a canonical basis of
\Hom(V_{2},A),
namely the elements

\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&a\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right),\quad\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&b\\
zy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right),\quad\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&0\\
xz&\mapsto&0\\
zy&\mapsto&c\end{array}\right),

with
a,b,c\in{\mathcal{B}},
we obtain the following conclusion.

Lemma B.6
.

The image of the differential
d_{3}^{*}
is generated over
\mathbf{k}
by the elements of the form
d_{3}^{*}(f),
where

f=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)x&\mapsto&a\\
xz&\mapsto&b\\
zy&\mapsto&c\end{array}\right)\in\Hom(V_{2},A)
runs over the following cases:

a=1,b=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&-z\\
xzy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=y^{n_{0}},b=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}}z\\
xzy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=y^{n_{0}}x,b=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}},b=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z\\
xzy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},b=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p-1}}xy^{n_{p}}z^{m_{p}+1}\\
xzy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,b=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},b=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}z\\
xzy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=x,b=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=xy^{n_{1}},b=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&-xy^{n_{1}}z\\
xzy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},b=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&-xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p-1}}xy^{n_{p}}z^{m_{p}+1}\\
xzy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,b=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},b=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&-xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}z\\
xzy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},b=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&-y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p-1}}xy^{n_{p}}z^{m_{p}+1}\\
xzy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,b=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},b=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&-y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}z\\
xzy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},b=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&-z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p-1}}xy^{n_{p}}z^{m_{p}+1}\\
xzy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,b=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

a=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},b=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&-z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}z\\
xzy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

b=1,a=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&x+y\\
xzy&\mapsto&-y\end{array}\right);

b=y^{n_{0}},a=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&xy^{n_{0}}+y^{n_{0}+1}\\
xzy&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}+1}\end{array}\right);

b=y^{n_{0}}x,a=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}}xy\end{array}\right);

b=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}},a=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}+1}\end{array}\right);

b=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},a=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

b=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,a=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy\end{array}\right);

b=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},a=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}+1}\end{array}\right);

b=x,a=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&-xy\end{array}\right);

b=xy^{n_{1}},a=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&-xy^{n_{1}+1}\end{array}\right);

b=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},a=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

b=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,a=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&-xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy\end{array}\right);

b=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},a=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&-xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}+1}\end{array}\right);

b=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},a=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}+y^{n_{1}+1}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}\\
xzy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

b=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,a=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x+y^{n_{1}+1}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\\
xzy&\mapsto&-y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy\end{array}\right);

b=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},a=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}+y^{n_{1}+1}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}\\
xzy&\mapsto&-y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}+1}\end{array}\right);

b=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},a=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&yz^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}\\
xzy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

b=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,a=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&yz^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\\
xzy&\mapsto&-z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy\end{array}\right);

b=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},a=c=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&yz^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}\\
xzy&\mapsto&-z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}+1}\end{array}\right);

c=1,a=b=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&x\end{array}\right);

c=y^{n_{0}},a=b=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&xy^{n_{0}}\end{array}\right);

c=y^{n_{0}}x,a=b=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}+1}x\end{array}\right);

c=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}},a=b=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}+1}xy^{n_{1}}\end{array}\right);

c=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},a=b=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}+1}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}\end{array}\right);

c=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,a=b=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}+1}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\end{array}\right);

c=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},a=b=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&-y^{n_{0}+1}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}\end{array}\right);

c=x,a=b=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&-yx\end{array}\right);

c=xy^{n_{1}},a=b=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&-yxy^{n_{1}}\end{array}\right);

c=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},a=b=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&-yxy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}\end{array}\right);

c=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,a=b=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&-yxy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\end{array}\right);

c=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},a=b=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&-yxy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}\end{array}\right);

c=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},a=b=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}\end{array}\right);

c=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,a=b=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x\end{array}\right);

c=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},a=b=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}\end{array}\right);

c=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},a=b=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

c=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,a=b=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right);

c=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},a=b=0,

d_{3}^{*}(f)=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right).

Here, the indices run over all integers
p\geqslant 1,
and
n_{0},n_{1},\cdots,n_{p+1},m_{1},\cdots,m_{p}\geqslant 1.

By computing the action of
d_{4}^{*}
on a canonical basis of
\Hom(V_{3},A),
namely the elements

\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&a\\
xzy&\mapsto&0\end{array}\right),\quad\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&0\\
xzy&\mapsto&b\end{array}\right),

with
a,b\in{\mathcal{B}},
we obtain the following conclusion.

Lemma B.7
.

The image of the differential
d_{4}^{*}
is generated over
\mathbf{k}
by the elements of the form
d_{4}^{*}(f),
where

f=\left(\begin{array}[]{rcl}(x+y)xz&\mapsto&a\\
xzy&\mapsto&b\end{array}\right)\in\Hom(V_{3},A)
runs over the following cases:

a=1,b=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto y;

a=y^{n_{0}},b=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto y^{n_{0}+1};

a=y^{n_{0}}x,b=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto y^{n_{0}}xy;

a=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}},b=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}+1};

a=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},b=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto 0;

a=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,b=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy;

a=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},b=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}+1};

a=x,b=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto xy;

a=xy^{n_{1}},b=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto xy^{n_{1}+1};

a=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},b=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto 0;

a=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,b=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy;

a=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},b=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}+1};

a=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},b=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto 0;

a=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,b=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy;

a=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},b=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}+1};

a=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},b=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto 0;

a=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,b=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy;

a=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},b=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}+1};

b=1,a=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto x+y;

b=y^{n_{0}},a=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto xy^{n_{0}}+y^{n_{0}+1};

b=y^{n_{0}}x,a=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto 0;

b=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}},a=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto 0;

b=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},a=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto 0;

b=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,a=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto 0;

b=y^{n_{0}}xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},a=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto 0;

b=x,a=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto 0;

b=xy^{n_{1}},a=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto 0;

b=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},a=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto 0;

b=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,a=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto 0;

b=xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},a=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto 0;

b=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},a=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}+y^{n_{1}+1}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}};

b=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,a=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x+y^{n_{1}+1}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x;

b=y^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},a=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto xy^{n_{1}}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}+y^{n_{1}+1}z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}};

b=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}},a=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto yz^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}};

b=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x,a=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto yz^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}x;

b=z^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}},a=0,

d_{4}^{*}(f):(x+y)xzy\mapsto yz^{m_{1}}\cdots z^{m_{p}}xy^{n_{p+1}}.

Here, the indices run over all integers
p\geqslant 1,
and
n_{0},n_{1},\cdots,n_{p+1},m_{1},\cdots,m_{p}\geqslant 1.
